Model before rewriting:

find m: matrix indexed by [int(-2, 3)] of int(-1, 2)
find b: matrix indexed by [int(1..2)] of bool

find j: int(1..3)
find r: bool

such that

r = m[i] = -1,
or([i = 4,r;int(1..)]),
or([j = 3,b[j];int(1..)]),
!(and([j = 1,!(b[j]);int(1..)])),
sum([m[-2],m[3];int(1..)]) = 1
